        /\b(but|however)\b[\s\S]*\b(snatches?|takes?|pulls?|knocks?|steals?|away|out of reach|runs away with it|slips away|dissolves|illusion|nothing)\b/.test(lower);
    },
    isInstructionalOrQuotedAcquisition: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      var hasAcq = /\b(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|take(?:s|n)?|grab(?:s|bed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?)\b/.test(lower);
      if (!hasAcq) return false;
      if (/\b(?:sign|notice|poster|book|manual|scroll|title|example|lesson|grammar|says?|said|reads?|read|shouts?|claims?|joke|rumor|rumour)\b/.test(lower) && /"[^\"]*\b(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|take(?:s|n)?|grab(?:s|bed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?)\b[^\"]*"/.test(lower)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:sign|notice|poster)\s+reads?\s*:/.test(lower)) return true;
      return false;
    },
    positiveAcquisitionSegments: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var pieces = c.split(/\b(?:but|however)\b/i);
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 1; i < pieces.length; i++) {
        var p = WL.U.norm(pieces[i]);
        if (/\b(?:hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?|places?|sets?|slips?|pick(?:s)? up|take(?:s)?|grab(?:s)?|receive(?:s)?|pocket(?:s)?|retrieve(?:s)?|recover(?:s)?|secure(?:s)?|snatch(?:es)? up|scoop(?:s)? up|catch(?:es)?)\b/.test(WL.U.lower(p))) out.push(p);
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectCurrencyWindowPayments: function (text) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(text);
      var out = [];
      var num = '(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)';
      var re = new RegExp("\\b(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}|he|she|they|the [a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\\s+counts?\\s+out\\s+" + num + "\\s+(?:dull\\s+|bright\\s+|old\\s+|small\\s+|worn\\s+){0,4}(copper|silver|gold)(?:\\s+coins?)?\\b[\\s\\S]{0,180}\\b(?:small\\s+|worn\\s+|leather\\s+)?pouch\\b[\\s\\S]{0,120}\\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\\s*(?:to\\s+you|you)\\b", 'i');
      var m = c.match(re);
      if (m) {
        var amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        if (amount && currency) out.push({ payer: 'unknown payer', amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: 'gain', container: 'pouch', confidence: 'confirmed_multiclause_currency_handoff' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectPurchaseCandidate: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:buy|bought|purchase|purchased)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)\s+for\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (!m) return null;
      var itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
      var amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(m[3] || 'gold');
      if (!itemName || !amount) return null;
      return { itemName: itemName, itemId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(itemName), amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: 'spend', confidence: 'confirmed_purchase' };
    },
    detectCurrencyPayment: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var m, payer = '', amount = 0, currency = '', direction = 'gain';
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50})\s+(?:pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?)\s+you\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
      if (m) { payer = WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:are\s+)?(?:paid|given|handed)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b(?:\s+by\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}))?/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = WL.U.title(m[3] || 'unknown payer'); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:take|pocket|receive|secure)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = 'self/scene'; }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}?)\b[\s\S]{0,90}\bcounts?\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(?:dull\s+|bright\s+|old\s+)?(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\s*(?:to\s+you|you)\b/i);
        if (m) { payer = WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:he|she|they|the\s+[a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\s+counts?\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(?:dull\s+|bright\s+|old\s+)?(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\s*(?:to\s+you|you)\b/i);
        if (m) { payer = 'unknown payer'; amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:count|counts|counted)\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = 'player'; direction = 'spend'; }
      }
      if (!amount || amount < 1 || !currency) return null;
      return { payer: payer, amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: direction, confidence: direction === 'spend' ? 'confirmed_currency_spend' : 'confirmed_payment_phrase' };
    },
    detectInventoryCandidate: function (clause) {
      var list = WL.EventScanner.detectInventoryCandidates(clause);
      return list.length ? list[0] : null;
    },
    detectInventoryCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      // Item loss / custody loss.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:drop|dropped|discard|discarded|throw|throws|threw|toss|tossed|hurl|hurled)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)(?:\s+(?:into|in|onto|on|at|toward|down|away|from)\b|[.!?;,]|$)/i);
      if (m) {
        var lost = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
        if (lost) out.push({ type: 'loss_candidate', targetName: lost, targetId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(lost), reason: 'dropped_or_thrown', confidence: 'confirmed_item_loss' });
        return out;
      }
      // Item damage / condition.
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?\b(?:sword|dagger|blade|key|shield|armor|lantern|ring|staff|bow|axe|spear))\s+(?:snaps?|breaks?|shatters?|cracks?|bends?|rusts?|burns?)\b(?:\s+(in half|apart|badly|completely))?/i);
      if (m) {
        var damaged = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
        if (damaged) out.push({ type: 'condition_candidate', targetName: damaged, targetId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(damaged), condition: /snap|break|shatter/i.test(c) ? 'broken' : 'damaged', confidence: 'confirmed_item_condition' });
        return out;
      }
      if (WL.EventScanner.detectCurrencyPayment(c) || WL.EventScanner.detectPurchaseCandidate(c)) return out;
      var segments = [c];
      if (WL.EventScanner.hasNegativeAcquisition(c)) {
        segments = WL.EventScanner.positiveAcquisitionSegments(c);
        if (!segments.length) return out;
      }
      var seen = {};
      for (var s = 0; s < segments.length; s++) {
        var seg = WL.U.norm(segments[s]);
        if (!seg || WL.EventScanner.hasNegativeAcquisition(seg)) continue;
        var m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:pick(?:s)? up|grab(?:s)?|take(?:s)? hold of|take(?:s)?|loot(?:s)?|receive(?:s)?|pocket(?:s)?|snatch(?:es)? up|scoop(?:s)? up|secure(?:s)?|retrieve(?:s)?|recover(?:s)?|lift(?:s)?|catch(?:es)?)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?)\s+you\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:places?|sets?|slips?)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100}?)\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\byou\s+(?:are\s+)?given\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) continue;
        var parts = WL.EventScanner.splitCandidateTargets(m2[1]);
        for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
          var target = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(parts[i]);
          if (!target) continue;
          var id = 'item_' + WL.U.slug(target);
          if (seen[id]) continue;
          seen[id] = true;
          out.push({ type: 'acquire_candidate', targetName: target, targetId: id, confidence: 'confirmed_acquisition_phrase' });
        }
      }
      return out;
    },
    splitCandidateTargets: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(target);
      if (!t) return [];
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:by|from)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}\s*$/i, '');
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:before|while|as|when|but|and then|from|off|out of|into|onto)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
      var raw = t.split(/\s*,\s*|\s+and\s+(?=(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?[a-z][a-z' -]{1,60}$)/i);
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
        var p = WL.U.norm(raw[i]).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '');
        if (p) out.push(p);
      }
      return out.length ? out : [t];
    },
    isAbstractInventoryTarget: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.lower(target);
      if (!t) return true;
      return /^(?:idea|thought|concept|plan|rumor|rumour|promise|quest|task|mission|objective|reward|payment|thanks|gratitude|advice|information|info|news|look|glance|stare|view|breath|moment|time|chance|opportunity|effort|pain|fear|hope|memory|dream|vision|attention|hold|bearings)$/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:idea|thought|concept|rumor|rumour|dream|vision|memory|notion)\s+of\b/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:long\s+)?look\s+(?:at|over|around)\b/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:your\s+)?bearings\b/i.test(t);
    },
    cleanCandidateTarget: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(target)
        .replace(/\s+(?:by|from)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\b(before|while|as|when|but|and then|from|off|out of)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/^hold\s+of\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^up\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (!t || t.length < 2) return '';
      if (WL.EventScanner.isAbstractInventoryTarget(t)) return '';
      if (/\b(quest|task|mission|objective|reward|promise)\b/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (/^(?:\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+(?:gold|silver|copper)(?:\s+coins?)?$/i.test(t)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(t);
    },
    numberWord: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value);
      var map = { a: 1, an: 1, one: 1, two: 2, three: 3, four: 4, five: 5, six: 6, seven: 7, eight: 8, nine: 9, ten: 10, eleven: 11, twelve: 12, thirteen: 13, fourteen: 14, fifteen: 15, sixteen: 16, seventeen: 17, eighteen: 18, nineteen: 19, twenty: 20, thirty: 30, forty: 40, fifty: 50, sixty: 60, seventy: 70, eighty: 80, ninety: 90 };
      if (/^\d+$/.test(v)) return Number(v);
      return map[v] || 0;
    },
    durationMinutes: function (amount, unit) {
      var n = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(amount);
      var u = WL.U.lower(unit);
      if (!n) return 0;
      if (/day/.test(u)) return n * 1440;
      if (/hour/.test(u)) return n * 60;
      return n;
    },
    cleanPlaceName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/["“”]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/^(?:at|in|into|inside|to|the)\s+/i, function (m) { return /^the\s+/i.test(m) ? 'The ' : ''; });
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:near|beside|by|on|in|at|with|where)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || raw.length < 3) return '';
      if (WL.EventScanner.isGenericPlaceName(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    isGenericPlaceName: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/, '');
      return /^(?:inn|tavern|street|road|door|counter|room|building|shop|market|square|town|city|village|guard|guards|prison|cell|forest|cave|house|home|wall|gate|alley|path|roadside)$/.test(v);
    },
    cleanActorName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:at|in|near|of|from)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:you|your|he|she|they|it)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      if (/^(?:guard|guards|soldier|soldiers|villager|villagers|people|crowd|goblins?|wolves?|bandits?)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    detectTimeTravelCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:road|route|path|trail|trip|journey)\s+from\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+to\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:takes?|lasts?|is)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var from = WL.U.title(m[1]);
        var to = WL.U.title(m[2]);
        var minutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[3], m[4]);
        if (from && to && minutes) out.push({ type: 'route_base_candidate', from: from, to: to, minutes: minutes, targetId: WL.EventScanner.routeId(from, to), confidence: 'confirmed_route_duration' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(rain|storm|mud|injury|detour|roadblock|fight|ambush|scouting|heavy burden|snow|fog|flood)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:trip|journey|route|travel|road)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:takes?|took|lasts?|lasted)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(rain|storm|mud|injury|detour|roadblock|fight|ambush|scouting|heavy burden|snow|fog|flood)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\bmakes?\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:trip|journey|route|travel)\s+take\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var delayMinutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[2], m[3]);
        if (delayMinutes) out.push({ type: 'route_delay_candidate', reason: WL.U.title(m[1]), actualMinutes: delayMinutes, confidence: 'confirmed_route_delay_reason' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\s+(?:pass|passes|passed|later)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:wait)\s+for\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var elapsed = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[1], m[2]);
        if (elapsed) out.push({ type: 'elapsed_time_candidate', minutes: elapsed, confidence: 'confirmed_elapsed_time' });
      }
      // Work/labor shifts: time advances, and body/fatigue handling is produced by FormTransformationSystem scanner.
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty)\s+(hours?)\s+pass\s+as\s+you\s+(?:work|labor|train|haul|march|dig|practice)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var workMinutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[1], m[2]);
        if (workMinutes && !out.some(function (e) { return e.type === 'elapsed_time_candidate' && e.minutes === workMinutes; })) out.push({ type: 'elapsed_time_candidate', minutes: workMinutes, confidence: 'confirmed_labor_elapsed_time' });
      }
      if (/\b(?:next day|the following day|tomorrow morning|next morning|the next morning)\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'next_day_candidate', hour: /dawn/i.test(c) ? 5 : 8, minute: 0, confidence: 'confirmed_next_day_language' });
      } else if (/\bbefore dawn\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'next_day_candidate', hour: 5, minute: 0, confidence: 'confirmed_before_dawn_language' });
      }
      if (WL.EventScanner.isRestRecoveryClause(c)) {
        var overnight = /\b(?:night|overnight|morning|dawn|next morning|before dawn)\b/i.test(c);
        var quality = /\b(?:safe|warm|comfortable|bed|inn|room|camp|shelter)\b/i.test(c) ? 'good' : 'ordinary';
        out.push({ type: 'rest_recovery_candidate', minutes: overnight ? 480 : 120, overnight: overnight, targetHour: /before dawn|dawn/i.test(c) ? 5 : 7, targetMinute: 0, restQuality: quality, hpRecover: quality === 'good' ? 25 : 18, mpRecover: quality === 'good' ? 20 : 12, epRecover: quality === 'good' ? 55 : 40, confidence: 'confirmed_rest_recovery' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:must|needs?|need|has to|have to|should)\b[\s\S]{0,100}\bby\s+(sunset|sunrise|dawn|dusk|midnight|noon|tomorrow|tonight|morning|evening|\d{1,2}(?::\d{2})?\s*(?:am|pm)?)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'deadline_candidate', label: WL.U.title(m[1]), task: WL.U.norm(c).slice(0, 120), confidence: 'confirmed_deadline_language' });
      return out;
    },
    isRestRecoveryClause: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      if (!/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:sleep|slept|rest|rested|recover|recovered)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (!/\b(?:night|overnight|morning|recover|recovering|recovered|resting|sleep|slept|safe|bed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:try|tries|tried|attempt|attempts|attempted|almost|cannot|can't|couldn't|fails?|failed)\b[\s\S]{0,50}\b(?:sleep|rest|recover)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:but|however)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:attack|attacks|ambush|interrupt|interrupted|wake|wakes|danger|enemy|monster|screams?|alarm|fire)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      // Place names like "Mourner's Rest" should not count without a sleep/rest verb.
      return true;
    },
    routeId: function (from, to) {
      var a = WL.U.title(from), b = WL.U.title(to);
      var pair = [a, b].sort();
      return 'route_' + WL.U.slug(pair[0] + '_to_' + pair[1]);
    },
    detectWorldPlaceCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/^\s*(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\s+is\s+(?:a|an|the)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,40}?)(?:\s+(near|in|on|at|beside|by)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,100}))?(?:[.!?])?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var placeName = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        var placeType = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        if (placeName && !WL.EventScanner.isGenericPlaceName(placeName) && !/^(?:left|right|north|south|east|west|front|rear|center|centre)$/.test(placeType) && !/^(?:counter|door|stairs|hearth|bar|gate|wall)$/.test(WL.U.lower(placeName))) {
          out.push({ type: 'place_definition_candidate', placeName: placeName, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(placeName), placeType: placeType, locationHint: WL.U.norm((m[3] || '') + ' ' + (m[4] || '')).trim(), confidence: 'confirmed_place_definition' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:enter|arrive at|reach|go to|return to|step into|walk into)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var current = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        if (current) out.push({ type: 'current_place_candidate', placeName: current, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(current), confidence: 'confirmed_current_place' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+owns\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var owner = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var ownedPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (owner && ownedPlace) out.push({ type: 'owner_relation_candidate', actorName: owner, placeName: ownedPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(ownedPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_place_owner' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:works at|works in|serves at|is employed at)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var worker = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var workPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (worker && workPlace) out.push({ type: 'worker_relation_candidate', actorName: worker, placeName: workPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(workPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_place_worker' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})'s\s+(common room|taproom|kitchen|cellar|upper room|barroom|hall|room)\b[\s\S]{0,120}/i);
      if (m) {
        var possessivePlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        if (possessivePlace) {
          out.push({ type: 'place_definition_candidate', placeName: possessivePlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace), placeType: 'building', locationHint: WL.U.title(m[2]), confidence: 'possessive_room_place_reference' });
          out.push({ type: 'room_relation_candidate', placeName: possessivePlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace), roomName: WL.U.title(m[2]), roomId: 'room_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace + '_' + m[2]), confidence: 'confirmed_room_reference' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(counter|stairs|door|hearth|bar|fountain|notice board|gate|cell door|window)\s+(?:is|are|sits|stands|lies)\s+(?:on|at|to|in)\s+(?:the\s+)?(left|right|north|south|east|west|center|centre|front|rear|back)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'layout_fact_candidate', fixture: WL.U.title(m[1]), value: WL.U.lower(m[2]).replace('centre', 'center'), confidence: 'confirmed_layout_fact' });
      return out;
    },
    detectActorSocialCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/^\s*(?:\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)?\s*(guards?|soldiers?|villagers?|people|crowd|bandits?|goblins?|wolves?)\b[\s\S]{0,120}/i);
      if (m) {
        out.push({ type: 'scene_extra_candidate', label: WL.U.title(m[1]), note: c.slice(0, 160), confidence: 'scene_extra_only' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(smiles?|frowns?|nods?|laughs?|glares?|swears?|promises?|thanks?|helps?|warns?)\b([\s\S]{0,140})/i);
      if (m) {
        var name = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var verb = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        var rest = WL.U.lower(m[3] || '');
        if (name) {
          var kind = /swears?/.test(verb) && /revenge|vengeance|kill|pay/i.test(rest) ? 'grudge' : (/smiles?|thanks?|helps?/.test(verb) ? 'positive_attitude' : (/glares?|frowns?|warns?/.test(verb) ? 'negative_attitude' : 'social_evidence'));
          out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: name, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(name), socialKind: kind, verb: verb, note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_named_actor_social' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+owns\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var owner = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var place = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (owner && place) out.push({ type: 'actor_place_relation_candidate', actorName: owner, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(owner), relation: 'owner', placeName: place, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(place), confidence: 'confirmed_actor_place_relation' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:works at|works in|serves at|is employed at)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var worker = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var workPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (worker && workPlace) out.push({ type: 'actor_place_relation_candidate', actorName: worker, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(worker), relation: 'worker', placeName: workPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(workPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_actor_place_relation' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60}?)\s+(?:watches?|counts?|pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave)\b([\s\S]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) {
        var evidenceActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (evidenceActor) out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: evidenceActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(evidenceActor), socialKind: /pay|paid|count|hand|give|gave/i.test(m[0]) ? 'transaction_evidence' : 'presence_evidence', verb: 'evidence', note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_named_actor_evidence' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+(?:asks?|asked|hires?|hired|offers?|offered|tasks?|contracts?)\s+you\s+(?:to|with|for)\s+([^.!?;]{3,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        var questGiverActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver(m[1]);
        if (questGiverActor && !/^(?:unknown)$/i.test(questGiverActor)) out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: questGiverActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(questGiverActor), socialKind: /guild|company|order|circle|clan|synod|dominion|consortium|covenant/i.test(questGiverActor) ? 'faction_or_institution_quest_evidence' : 'quest_giver_evidence', verb: 'quest_offer', note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_giver_evidence' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    cleanCreatureLabel: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|remaining)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:attack|attacks|charge|charges|march|marches|advance|advances|appear|appears|surround|surrounds|approach|approaches|die|dies|fall|falls|are defeated|is defeated|flee|fleeing|surrender|surrenders|are dead|are killed)[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    singularCreature: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|remaining)\s+/, '').trim();
      if (/ies$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/ies$/, 'y');
      else if (/ves$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/ves$/, 'f');
      else if (/s$/.test(v) && !/ss$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/s$/, '');
      return WL.U.title(v);
    },
    detectCombatThreatCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:join|joins|arrive|arrives|reinforce|reinforces|enter|enters)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:fight|battle|combat|fray)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var addCount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var addLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (addCount > 0 && addLabel) out.push({ type: 'reinforcement_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(addLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(addLabel), count: addCount, confidence: 'confirmed_group_reinforcement' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:attack|attacks|charge|charges|march|marches|advance|advances|surround|surrounds|appear|appears|approach|approaches)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var count = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var label = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (count > 0 && label) out.push({ type: 'group_count_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(label), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(label), count: count, confidence: 'confirmed_group_count' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:a|an|the)\s+(horde|pack|swarm|mob|band|group)\s+of\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,80}?)\s+(?:attack|attacks|surround|surrounds|appear|appears|emerge|emerges|approach|approaches|descend|descends)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var groupWord = WL.U.lower(m[1]);
        var packLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        var estimate = /horde|swarm/.test(groupWord) ? 20 : (/pack|band|group/.test(groupWord) ? 6 : 10);
        if (packLabel) {
          out.push({ type: 'group_count_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(packLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(packLabel), count: estimate, estimated: true, groupWord: groupWord, confidence: 'estimated_group_pressure' });
          out.push({ type: 'threat_profile_candidate', creatureName: WL.U.title(packLabel), threatId: 'threat_' + WL.U.slug(packLabel), confidence: 'group_threat_presence' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:die|dies|are killed|are slain|fall|falls)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var casualties = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var deadLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (casualties > 0 && deadLabel) out.push({ type: 'casualty_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(deadLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(deadLabel), casualties: casualties, confidence: 'confirmed_group_casualties' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:all|the remaining|the rest of the|the rest of)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:are|is)?\s*(defeated|dead|killed|slain|gone|fleeing|flee|flee\b|surrendering|surrender)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var allLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
        if (allLabel) out.push({ type: 'all_defeated_claim', groupName: WL.U.title(allLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(allLabel), resolution: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: /rest|remaining/.test(WL.U.lower(c)) ? 'confirmed_group_resolution' : 'claimed_group_resolution' });
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:remaining|rest of the|rest of)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(flee|fleeing|surrender|surrendering)\b/i);
        if (m) {
          var fleeingLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
          if (fleeingLabel) out.push({ type: 'all_defeated_claim', groupName: WL.U.title(fleeingLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(fleeingLabel), resolution: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: 'confirmed_group_resolution' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:a|an|the)\s+((?:(?:ancient|legendary|inferno|fire|large|city|village|elite|veteran|captain|true|greater|lesser|normal)\s+)*(?:dragon|demon|wolf|wolves|rat|guard|soldier|goblin|orc|troll|beast|monster|wraith|spirit|giant|drake|wyvern))\s+(?:attacks|appears|emerges|lunges|blocks|approaches|waits|guards|meets|faces|surrounds)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:a\s+level\s+\d+\s+player|level\s+\d+\s+player|you|the player)\s+(?:meets?|faces?|encounters?)\s+(?:a|an|the)?\s*([a-z][a-z -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var creature = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
        if (creature && !/^(?:idea|look|effort|quest|reward)$/i.test(creature)) out.push({ type: 'threat_profile_candidate', creatureName: WL.U.title(creature), threatId: 'threat_' + WL.U.slug(creature), confidence: 'confirmed_threat_presence' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    cleanAdvancedName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:from|with|using|by|near|at|in)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:it|this|that|something|anything)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    detectAdvancedBoundaryCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)\s+costs?\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'economy_price_quote_candidate', itemName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), amount: Number(m[2]), currency: WL.U.lower(m[3]), confidence: 'confirmed_price_quote' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+owe\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+to\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'economy_debt_candidate', amount: Number(m[1]), currency: WL.U.lower(m[2]), creditor: WL.U.title(m[3]), confidence: 'confirmed_debt' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:learn|learned|discover|discovered)\s+(?:the\s+)?recipe\s+for\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'crafting_recipe_candidate', recipeName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), recipeId: 'recipe_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_recipe_knowledge' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:construction|work|repair|building)\s+(?:begins|started|starts|continues)\s+(?:on|at|for)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,100})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'domain_project_candidate', projectName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), projectId: 'project_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_project' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:an\s+)?army\s+of\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50})\s+(?:march|marches|moves|advances|gathers)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var count = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var forceName = WL.U.title(m[2]);
        if (count > 0) out.push({ type: 'warfare_force_candidate', forceName: forceName, forceId: 'force_' + WL.U.slug(forceName), count: count, confidence: 'confirmed_force_count' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:die|are killed|fall)\s+(?:in|during|to|from)\s+(?:the\s+)?(ambush|battle|war|fight|skirmish)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'warfare_casualty_candidate', forceName: WL.U.title(m[2]), forceId: 'force_' + WL.U.slug(m[2]), casualties: WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]), reason: WL.U.lower(m[3]), confidence: 'confirmed_warfare_casualties' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+absorb\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'absorption_candidate', sourceName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), candidateId: 'absorb_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), confidence: 'bounded_absorption_evidence' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+evolve\s+into\s+(?:an?\s+|the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'evolution_candidate', targetForm: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), candidateId: 'evolve_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), confidence: 'bounded_evolution_evidence' });
      return out.filter(function (e) { return !(e.itemName === '' || e.recipeName === '' || e.projectName === '' || e.sourceName === '' || e.targetForm === ''); });
    },
    detectFormBodyCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      function cleanForm(v) { return WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(v).replace(/^(?:a|an|the)\s+/i, '').replace(/\s+(?:form|body)$/i, '')); }
      function injurySeverity() {
        if (/\b(?:severe|terrible|deep|gaping|crippling|crushed|shattered|broken bone|spurts?|gushes?|badly)\b/.test(lower)) return 'severe';
        if (/\b(?:blood|bleeding|burns?|poison|venom|limps?|sprain|deep)\b/.test(lower)) return 'moderate';
        return 'minor';
      }
      function damageFor(sev) { return sev === 'severe' ? 16 : (sev === 'moderate' ? 8 : 4); }
      if (/\b(dream|vision|nightmare)\b/.test(lower)) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into|looks? like)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
        if (m) out.push({ type: /\bvision\b/.test(lower) ? 'vision_form_candidate' : 'dream_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'non_canon_dream_or_vision' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:disguise(?:s)? yourself as|disguised as|pretend(?:s)? to be)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'disguise_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_disguise_only' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:illusion|glamor|glamour)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:look(?:s)? like|appear(?:s)? as|seem(?:s)? to be)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'illusion_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_illusion_only' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:temporarily|for now|for a moment|briefly)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'temporary_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_temporary_form' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:permanently|forever|irreversibly)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into|changes? into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'permanent_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_permanent_form' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:your\s+)?(?:race|species)\s+(?:changes?|shifts?)\s+(?:to|into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'race_shift_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_race_shift' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:grow|sprout|tear|burst|emerge|form)\b|\b(?:grow|sprout|tear|burst|emerge|form)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var traitMatch = c.match(/\b(wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b/i);
        out.push({ type: 'body_trait_candidate', trait: traitMatch ? cleanForm(traitMatch[1]) : 'Body Mutation', confidence: 'confirmed_body_trait' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:cuts?|slashes?|stabs?|bites?|burns?|poisons?|breaks?|crushes?|shatters?)\s+(?:your\s+)?([a-z][a-z -]{1,40})\b|\byour\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,40})\s+(?:is|are)\s+(cut|slashed|stabbed|bitten|burned|poisoned|broken|crushed|shattered)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var loc = cleanForm(m[1] || m[2] || 'body');
        var sev = injurySeverity();
        if (/poison|venom|spider/.test(lower)) out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: 'poisoned', severity: sev === 'minor' ? 'moderate' : sev, hpDamage: 4, duration: 'until_treated_or_recovered', confidence: 'confirmed_poison_condition' });
        else out.push({ type: 'body_injury_candidate', injury: loc + ' wound', location: loc, severity: sev, hpDamage: damageFor(sev), confidence: 'confirmed_body_injury' });
      } else if ((/\bpoison\b|\bvenom\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:burns?|courses?|spreads?|in your veins|bites?)\b/.test(lower)) || /\bspider\s+bites?\s+you\b[\s\S]{0,80}\bpoison\b/i.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: 'poisoned', severity: 'moderate', hpDamage: 4, duration: 'until_treated_or_recovered', confidence: 'confirmed_poison_condition' });
      }
      if (/\b(?:hours?|day|shift)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:work|labor|haul|dig|march|train|practice|carry|lifting|docks?)\b|\b(?:work|labor|haul|dig|march|train|practice|carry|lifting)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:hours?|shift|until exhausted|tired|fatigue)\b/i.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: /\b(?:exhausted|exhaustion)\b/.test(lower) ? 'exhausted' : 'fatigued', severity: /\b(?:seven|eight|nine|ten|twelve|day|all day|exhausted)\b/.test(lower) ? 'moderate' : 'minor', epDamage: /\b(?:seven|eight|nine|ten|twelve|day|all day|exhausted)\b/.test(lower) ? 18 : 8, duration: 'until_rest', confidence: 'confirmed_fatigue_or_strain' });
      }
      return out;
    },

    cleanAbilityName: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.norm(name)
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:as\s+you\s+level\s+up|through\s+training|from\s+practice)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:again|carefully|successfully|hard|under pressure)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\b(?:spell|technique|skill|ability|passive|talent)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (!n || n.length < 2) return '';
      if (/^(?:sword|dagger|coin|key|ring|lantern|map|potion|quest|task|mission)$/i.test(n)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(n);
    },
    detectAbilityProgressionCandidates: function (clause, hookKind) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m, name;
      if (/\b(?:only read the title|learn nothing|no usable technique remains|do not know that technique|don't know that technique|pretend to use|joking|as a joke|manual says|scroll title says)\b/i.test(lower)) return out;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:gain|gained|learn|learned|unlock|unlocked|acquire|acquired)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:skill|technique|ability)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})(?:\s+as\s+you\s+level\s+up|\s+through\s+training|\s+from\s+practice)?/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'skill_gain_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), category: 'skill', confidence: 'explicit_story_skill_gain' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'skill', amount: 5, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'explicit_skill_gain', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:set|add|update)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+keys?\s*[:=]\s*([^.!?]{1,180})/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+keys?\s+(?:are|as)\s+([^.!?]{1,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        var keys = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.cleanKeys ? WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.cleanKeys(m[2]) : String(m[2] || '').split(/[,;|]/);
        if (name && keys.length) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'keys_update_request', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), keys: keys, confidence: hookKind === 'input' ? 'player_management_request' : 'story_keys_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      if (hookKind === 'output') {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:overcharge|overcharges)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:until|beyond|past|with|through|so)\b|[.!?;,]|$)/i);
        if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:push|pushes|force|forces)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:beyond|past|over|through)\s+(?:its\s+|your\s+)?(?:limit|limits|maximum|safe limit)/i);
        if (m) {
          name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
          if (name) {
            var hpRisk = /blood|life force|lifeforce|sacrifice|vein|heart|flesh|body burns|burns inside|burning inside/.test(lower) ? 4 : 0;
            var mpRisk = /mana|mind|focus|headache|arcane|spell|magic/.test(lower) ? 3 : 1;
            var epRisk = /strain|fatigue|muscle|breath|exhaust|body/.test(lower) ? 3 : 1;
            out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'cost_pressure_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), riskWords: c, hpRisk: hpRisk, mpRisk: mpRisk, epRisk: epRisk, confidence: 'observed_overcharge_cost_pressure' });
          }
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:make|set|classify|treat)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:(?:as|into|to be)\s+)?(?:a\s+|an\s+)?(skill|passive|talent|ability)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'category_transfer_request', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), category: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: hookKind === 'input' ? 'player_management_request' : 'story_category_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:try|tries|attempt|attempts)\s+to\s+(?:cast|use|perform|activate|shape)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s*,?\s*(?:but|and)\s+|\s+but\b|\s+fails?\b|\s+fizzles?\b|\s+sputters?\b)/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'failed_attempt_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'failed_attempt_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:successfully\s+)?(?:cast|casts|perform|performs|activate|activates)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'ability_evidence_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'successful_use_evidence' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 5, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'successful_ability_use', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:practice|practices|train|trains|trained)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'training_evidence_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'training_evidence' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 3, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'training_or_practice', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:study|studies|studied)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 2, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: { int: 2 }, reason: 'study_or_theory', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      }
      if (/\binspect(?:s|ed)?\b/.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'ability_evidence_candidate', abilityName: 'Inspection', targetName: 'Inspection', targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId('Inspection'), confidence: 'practical_ability_use' });
        out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 2, domains: ['investigation'], stats: { int: 1, lck: 1 }, reason: 'inspection_use', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      }
      if (/\b(sprint|dash|dodge|evade|outrun)\b/.test(lower)) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'stat', amount: 2, domains: ['mobility'], stats: { spd: 3 }, reason: 'mobility_pressure', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      if (/\b(block|endure|withstand|brace)\b/.test(lower)) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'stat', amount: 2, domains: ['defense'], stats: { def: 3 }, reason: 'defensive_pressure', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      return out;
    },
    statXpForAbility: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name);
      if (/fire|spell|magic|rune|inspect|study/.test(n)) return { int: 2 };
      if (/sword|slash|blade/.test(n)) return { atk: 2, spd: 1 };
      if (/box|punch|fist/.test(n)) return { atk: 1, def: 1 };
      if (/regen|heal/.test(n)) return { def: 1 };
      return {};
    },
    detectQuestCandidate: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var m, giver, objective, reward;
      // Build 7.4: quest offers may come from named people, organizations, or generic institutions like the guild.
      m = c.match(/^((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+(?:asks?|asked|hires?|hired|tasks?|contracts?|contracted|charges?)\s+you\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220}?)(?:\s+and\s+promises?\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/^((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+offers?\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:a\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220}?)(?:\s+for\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m) {
        giver = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver(m[1]);
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[2]);
        reward = m[3] ? { amount: Number(m[3]), currency: WL.U.lower(m[4] || 'gold'), payer: giver } : null;
        return { type: 'offer_candidate', giver: giver, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), reward: reward, links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, giver), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_offer' };
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|agree\s+to|agreed\s+to|take|took)\s+((?:[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})'?s)?\s*(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})[.!?]?/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|agree\s+to|agreed\s+to|take|took)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})/i);
      if (m) {
        if (m.length >= 3) { giver = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver((m[1] || '').replace(/'s$/i, '')); objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[2]); }
        else { giver = ''; objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[1]); }
        return { type: 'accept_candidate', giver: giver, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, giver), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_acceptance' };
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:clear|cleared|finish|finished|complete|completed|resolve|resolved|defeat|defeated|drive off|drove off|repair|repaired|escort|escorted|deliver|delivered|recover|recovered|retrieve|retrieved|bring|brought|find|found|investigate|investigated|protect|protected)\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})/i);
      if (m) {
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[1]);
        return { type: 'status_candidate', status: 'ready_to_turn_in', objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, ''), confidence: 'objective_progress_or_completion_language' };
      }
      if (/\b(quest|task|mission|contract)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(complete|completed|finished|failed|abandoned|ready|turned in|turn in)\b/.test(lower)) {
        var status = /fail|abandon/.test(lower) ? 'failed' : (/turn(ed)? in|complete|completed/.test(lower) ? 'completed' : 'ready_to_turn_in');
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(c);
        return { type: 'status_candidate', status: status, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, ''), confidence: 'quest_status_language' };
      }
      return null;
    },
    cleanQuestGiver: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the\s+)/i, '');
      if (!raw) return 'Unknown';
      if (/^guild$/i.test(raw)) return 'Guild';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    extractQuestObjectiveLinks: function (objective, giver) {
      var o = WL.U.norm(objective);
      var links = { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      function add(list, name, kind) {
        name = WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(name).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, ''));
        if (!name || name.length < 2) return;
        var id = (kind || 'ref') + '_' + WL.U.slug(name);
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].id === id) return;
        list.push({ id: id, name: name, kind: kind || 'ref' });
      }
      var m;
      m = o.match(/\bescort\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90})\b/i);
      if (m) { add(links.actors, m[1], 'actor'); add(links.places, m[2], 'place'); }
      m = o.match(/\b(?:meet|find|protect|rescue)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})(?:\s+(?:at|in|near|from)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}))?/i);
      if (m) { add(links.actors, m[1], 'actor'); if (m[2]) add(links.places, m[2], 'place'); }
      m = o.match(/\b(?:repair|rebuild|reinforce|inspect|investigate|protect)\s+(?:the\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,80}?\b(?:wall|gate|bridge|road|tower|well|house|farm|barrow|tavern|inn|hall|market|prison))\b/i);
      if (m) add(links.places, m[1], 'place');
      m = o.match(/\b(?:recover|retrieve|find|fetch|bring)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:from|at|in|inside|near)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}))?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var item = WL.U.norm(m[1]).replace(/\s+(?:from|at|in|inside|near)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
        if (item && !/^(?:your|his|her|their)\s/i.test(item)) add(links.items, item, 'objective_item');
        if (m[2]) add(links.places, m[2], 'place');
      }
      if (giver && /guild|company|circle|clan|order|synod|dominion|consortium|covenant|faction/i.test(giver)) add(links.factions, giver, 'faction');
      return links;
    },
    cleanQuestObjective: function (objective) {
      var o = WL.U.norm(objective)
        .replace(/\s+and\s+promises?\s+\d{1,6}\s+(?:copper|silver|gold)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+for\s+\d{1,6}\s+(?:copper|silver|gold)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission)\s+to\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^resolve\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      return o || 'unspecified objective';
    },
    makeQuestTitle: function (objective) {
      var o = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(objective);
      o = o.replace(/^(?:to\s+)?/i, '');
      return WL.U.title(o).replace(/^(?:Resolve\s+)+/i, '') || 'Untitled Quest';
    },
    record: function (root, events) {
      for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) root.diagnostics.parser.push(events[i]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.parser, WL.Constants.maxParserHits);
    }
  };

  WL.ProposalBuilder = {
    fromEvents: function (root, events, source) {
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) {
        var ev = events[i];
        var target = ev.data && (ev.data.targetId || ev.data.targetName || ev.data.objective || ev.data.title || ev.data.currency || ev.type) || '';
        var targetSlug = ev.data && ev.data.targetId ? ev.data.targetId : WL.U.slug(target);
        var localKey = [ev.family, ev.type, ev.sourceHash, ev.localClauseHash, targetSlug].join('|');
        if (seen[localKey]) continue;
        seen[localKey] = true;
        var proposal = {
          id: 'proposal_' + WL.U.hash(localKey + '|' + i),
          family: ev.family,
          type: ev.type,
          sourceHook: ev.sourceHook,
          sourceKind: ev.sourceKind,
          sourceHash: ev.sourceHash,
          localClauseHash: ev.localClauseHash,
          targetId: targetSlug,
          targetName: ev.data && ev.data.targetName || ev.data && ev.data.title || '',
          textPreview: ev.textPreview,
          data: ev.data || {},
          status: 'proposed',
          opKey: WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([ev.sourceHook, ev.sourceHash, ev.localClauseHash, ev.family, ev.type, targetSlug || 'none'])
        };
        out.push(proposal);
        root.diagnostics.proposals.push(proposal);
      }
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.proposals, WL.Constants.maxProposals);
      return out;
    }
  };

  WL.CanonValidator = {
    validateAll: function (root, proposals, source) {
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < proposals.length; i++) out.push(WL.CanonValidator.validate(root, proposals[i], source));
      for (var j = 0; j < out.length; j++) root.diagnostics.validations.push(out[j]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.validations, WL.Constants.maxValidations);
      return out;
    },
    validate: function (root, proposal, source) {
      var result = {
        id: 'validation_' + WL.U.hash(proposal.id + '|' + proposal.opKey),
        proposalId: proposal.id,
        proposal: proposal,
        family: proposal.family,
        type: proposal.type,
        accepted: false,
        status: 'REJECTED',
        reason: '',
        reducer: ''
      };
      var isAbilityManagementInput = source && source.kind === 'input' && proposal.family === 'ability' && (proposal.type === 'category_transfer_request' || proposal.type === 'keys_update_request');
      if ((!source || !source.eligibleForCanon) && !isAbilityManagementInput) {
        result.status = source && source.kind === 'input' ? 'PENDING' : 'REJECTED';
        result.reason = source && source.reason || 'source_not_eligible';
        return result;
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'system' && proposal.type === 'output_observed') {
        result.accepted = true;
        result.status = 'ACCEPTED';
        result.reason = 'pipeline_observation_only';
        result.reducer = 'SystemObservationReducer';
        return result;
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'inventory') {
        if (proposal.type === 'acquire_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.targetName) {
          if (WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem && WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.isProtectedObjectiveItem && WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.isProtectedObjectiveItem(root, proposal.data.targetName, proposal.textPreview)) {
            result.status = 'REJECTED'; result.reason = 'quest_objective_item_not_confirmed_possession'; return result;
          }
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_output_acquisition'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
        if ((proposal.type === 'payment_candidate' || proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate') && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0 && proposal.data.currency) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate' ? 'confirmed_purchase' : 'confirmed_output_payment'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
        if ((proposal.type === 'loss_candidate' || proposal.type === 'condition_candidate') && proposal.data && proposal.data.targetName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_inventory_state_change'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'quest') {
        if (proposal.type === 'offer_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.objective) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_offer'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'accept_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.objective) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_acceptance'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'reward_payment_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_reward_payment_record'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'status_candidate' && proposal.data && (proposal.data.objective || proposal.data.status)) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_progress_or_status'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'form') {
        if (proposal.data && (proposal.data.targetForm || proposal.data.trait || proposal.data.injury || proposal.data.condition)) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_form_or_body_evidence'; result.reducer = 'FormTransformationReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'ability') {
        if (proposal.type === 'category_transfer_request' && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = isAbilityManagementInput ? 'ACCEPTED_MANAGEMENT_INPUT' : 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'validated_ability_category_transfer'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'keys_update_request' && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName && proposal.data.keys && proposal.data.keys.length) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = isAbilityManagementInput ? 'ACCEPTED_MANAGEMENT_INPUT' : 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'validated_player_authored_keys_update'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'skill_gain_candidate' && source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'explicit_story_skill_gain'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'cost_pressure_candidate' && source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_overcharge_cost_pressure'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_ability_evidence'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'progression') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.type === 'xp_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_output_progression'; result.reducer = 'ProgressionReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'time') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_time_route_deadline_evidence'; result.reducer = 'TimeTravelReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'place') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_world_place_evidence'; result.reducer = 'WorldStructureReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'actor') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_actor_social_evidence'; result.reducer = 'ActorSocialReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'combat') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_combat_threat_evidence'; result.reducer = 'CombatThreatReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'advanced') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_advanced_boundary_evidence'; result.reducer = 'AdvancedBoundaryReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      result.status = 'PENDING';
      result.reason = 'no_owner_for_family_in_build7';
      return result;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry = {
    handlers: {},
    applyAcceptedBatch: function (root, validations, source) {
      var results = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < validations.length; i++) {
        var v = validations[i];
        if (!v.accepted) {
          results.push({ status: v.status, applied: false, reason: v.reason, family: v.family, type: v.type, targetId: v.proposal && v.proposal.targetId || '' });
          continue;
        }
        var key = v.proposal.opKey;
        var ledger = WL.OperationLedger.add(root, key, v.reducer || v.family);
        if (!ledger.applied) {
          results.push({ status: 'DUPLICATE_SKIPPED', applied: false, duplicate: true, key: ledger.key, family: v.family, type: v.type });
          continue;
        }
        var handler = WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers[v.reducer];
        if (typeof handler !== 'function') {
          results.push({ status: 'NO_HANDLER', applied: false, key: ledger.key, family: v.family, type: v.type, reason: 'handler_missing' });
          continue;
        }
        var h = handler(root, v, ledger);
        results.push(h);
      }
      for (var j = 0; j < results.length; j++) root.diagnostics.reducers.push(results[j]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.reducers, WL.Constants.maxReducerResults);
      return results;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.SystemObservationReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return {
      status: 'APPLIED_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY',
      applied: true,
      family: validation.family,
      type: validation.type,
      key: ledger.key,
      note: 'Build 6 commits diagnostics and installed owner reducers only.'
    };
  };

  WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var proposal = validation.proposal;
      if (proposal.type === 'acquire_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.acquire(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'payment_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.pay(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.purchase(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'loss_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.lose(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'condition_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.setCondition(root, proposal, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: validation.family, type: validation.type, reason: 'unsupported_inventory_event' };
    },
    isStackable: function (name) {
      return /\b(coin|coins|arrow|arrows|bolt|bolts|ration|rations|herb|herbs|potion|potions)\b/i.test(name || '');
    },
    findItemId: function (root, name) {
      var id = 'item_' + WL.U.slug(name || '');
      if (root.inventory.itemsById[id]) return id;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById || {});
      var slug = WL.U.slug(name || '');
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.inventory.itemsById[keys[i]];
        var rslug = WL.U.slug((rec && rec.displayName) || keys[i]);
        if (rslug === slug || rslug.indexOf(slug) >= 0 || slug.indexOf(rslug) >= 0) return keys[i];
      }
      return id;
    },
    addItemRecord: function (root, id, name, ledger, phrase, sourceHash) {
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) {
        item = { id: id, displayName: name, quantity: 1, stackable: WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.isStackable(name), condition: 'intact', custody: 'owned_by_player', equippedSlot: '', sourceHistory: [] };
        root.inventory.itemsById[id] = item;
        if (root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id);
      } else {
        item.custody = 'owned_by_player';
        if (root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id);
        if (item.stackable) item.quantity = WL.U.clamp(Number(item.quantity || 1) + 1, 1, 999999);
      }
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, sourceHash: sourceHash || '', phrase: phrase || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      return item;
    },
    acquire: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.targetName || proposal.targetName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = proposal.data.targetId || ('item_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      if (!name || /\bquest\b/i.test(name)) return { status: 'REJECTED_BAD_ITEM_NAME', applied: false, family: 'inventory', type: 'acquire_candidate', key: ledger.key };
      WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.addItemRecord(root, id, name, ledger, proposal.textPreview, proposal.sourceHash);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'acquire', itemId: id, name: name, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'acquire', itemId: id, targetName: name, key: ledger.key };
    },
    pay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.amount, 1, 999999);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.currency || 'gold');
      var direction = proposal.data.direction || 'gain';
      if (!root.inventory.currency[currency] && root.inventory.currency[currency] !== 0) root.inventory.currency[currency] = 0;
      if (direction === 'spend' || direction === 'loss') root.inventory.currency[currency] = Math.max(0, root.inventory.currency[currency] - amount);
      else root.inventory.currency[currency] += amount;
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: direction === 'spend' ? 'currency_spend' : 'payment', amount: amount, currency: currency, payer: proposal.data.payer || '', key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: direction === 'spend' ? 'currency_spend' : 'payment', amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key };
    },
    purchase: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.amount, 1, 999999);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.currency || 'gold');
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.itemName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = proposal.data.itemId || ('item_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      if (!root.inventory.currency[currency] && root.inventory.currency[currency] !== 0) root.inventory.currency[currency] = 0;
      if (root.inventory.currency[currency] < amount) {
        root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases.push({ key: ledger.key, itemName: name, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: 'insufficient_funds' });
        WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases, 40);
        return { status: 'BLOCKED_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'purchase', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
      }
      root.inventory.currency[currency] -= amount;
      WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.addItemRecord(root, id, name, ledger, proposal.textPreview, proposal.sourceHash);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'purchase', itemId: id, name: name, amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'purchase', itemId: id, amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key };
    },
    lose: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.findItemId(root, name);
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) return { status: 'NO_MATCHING_ITEM', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'loss', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
      var idx = root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id);
      if (idx >= 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.splice(idx, 1);
      item.custody = 'lost_or_discarded';
      item.lastLossReason = proposal.data.reason || 'lost';
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, action: 'loss' });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'loss', itemId: id, name: item.displayName, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'loss', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    setCondition: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.findItemId(root, name);
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) {
        item = { id: id, displayName: name, quantity: 1, stackable: false, condition: 'unknown', custody: 'not_confirmed_owned', equippedSlot: '', sourceHistory: [] };
        root.inventory.itemsById[id] = item;
      }
      item.condition = proposal.data.condition || 'damaged';
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, action: 'condition', condition: item.condition });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'condition', itemId: id, condition: item.condition, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'condition', itemId: id, condition: item.condition, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < root.inventory.ownedIds.length; i++) {
        var id = root.inventory.ownedIds[i];
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
        if (item) lines.push(item.displayName + ' x' + (item.quantity || 1) + ' [' + (item.condition || 'unknown') + ']');
      }
      if (!lines.length) lines.push('(none)');
      lines.push('Currency: ' + (root.inventory.currency.gold || 0) + ' gold, ' + (root.inventory.currency.silver || 0) + ' silver, ' + (root.inventory.currency.copper || 0) + ' copper');
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var proposal = validation.proposal;
      if (proposal.type === 'offer_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.offer(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'accept_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.accept(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'reward_payment_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.recordPayment(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'status_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.status(root, proposal, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: validation.family, type: validation.type, reason: 'unsupported_quest_event' };
    },
    questId: function (giver, objective) {
      return 'quest_' + WL.U.slug((giver || 'unknown') + '_' + (objective || 'objective'));
    },
    findByObjective: function (root, giver, objective) {
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.questId(giver || '', objective || '');
      if (root.quests.questsById[id]) return id;
      var targetSlug = WL.U.slug(objective || '');
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        if (WL.U.slug(q.objective || '') === targetSlug && (!giver || !q.giver || WL.U.slug(q.giver) === WL.U.slug(giver))) return keys[i];
      }
      return '';
    },
    ensureQuest: function (root, data) {
      var giver = WL.U.title(data.giver || 'Unknown');
      var objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(data.objective || data.title || 'unspecified objective');
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.findByObjective(root, giver, objective) || WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.questId(giver, objective);
      var q = root.quests.questsById[id];
      if (!q) {
        q = { id: id, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), giver: giver, objective: objective, status: 'offered', rewardsPromised: [], rewardsPaid: [], history: [], progress: [], objectiveLinks: { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] }, actorRefs: [], placeRefs: [], itemRefs: [], factionRefs: [], relationships: [] };
        root.quests.questsById[id] = q;
      }
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      return q;
    },
    offer: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var q = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuest(root, proposal.data);
      q.status = q.status || 'offered';
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      if (root.quests.offeredIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0 && root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0) root.quests.offeredIds.push(q.id);
      if (proposal.data.reward && proposal.data.reward.amount > 0) {
        var rid = 'reward_' + WL.U.hash(q.id + '|' + proposal.data.reward.amount + '|' + proposal.data.reward.currency);
        var exists = q.rewardsPromised.some(function (r) { return r.id === rid; });
        if (!exists) {
          var rec = { id: rid, questId: q.id, amount: proposal.data.reward.amount, currency: proposal.data.reward.currency, payer: proposal.data.reward.payer || q.giver, paid: false };
          q.rewardsPromised.push(rec);
          root.quests.rewardPromises.push(rec);
        }
      }
      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'offer', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'offer', questId: q.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    accept: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var q = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuest(root, proposal.data);
      q.status = 'active';
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      var oi = root.quests.offeredIds.indexOf(q.id);
      if (oi >= 0) root.quests.offeredIds.splice(oi, 1);
      if (root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0) root.quests.activeIds.push(q.id);
      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'accept', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'accept', questId: q.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    objectiveScore: function (a, b) {
      var stop = { the:1, a:1, an:1, to:1, from:1, his:1, her:1, their:1, old:1, bren:1, s:1, of:1, in:1, on:1, at:1, and:1, you:1 };
      function toks(v) { return WL.U.lower(v).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(function (x) { return x && !stop[x]; }); }
      var ta = toks(a), tb = toks(b), score = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < ta.length; i++) if (tb.indexOf(ta[i]) >= 0) score++;
      return score;
    },
    findBestByText: function (root, objective) {
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      var best = '', bestScore = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        var s = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.objectiveScore(q.objective || '', objective || '');
        if (s > bestScore) { best = keys[i]; bestScore = s; }
      }
      return bestScore >= 2 ? best : '';
    },
    status: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var wanted = proposal.data.status || 'ready_to_turn_in';
      var objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(proposal.data.objective || proposal.textPreview || '');
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.findBestByText(root, objective);
      if (!id) return { status: 'NO_MATCHING_QUEST', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'status', key: ledger.key };
      var q = root.quests.questsById[id];
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      q.status = wanted;
      q.progress = q.progress || [];
      q.progress.push({ key: ledger.key, status: wanted, objectiveEvidence: objective, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
