      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'status', status: wanted, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.progress, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.history, 30);
      if ((wanted === 'completed' || wanted === 'ready_to_turn_in') && root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(id) >= 0 && wanted === 'completed') root.quests.activeIds.splice(root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(id), 1);
      if (wanted === 'completed' && root.quests.completedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.quests.completedIds.push(id);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'status', questId: id, questStatus: wanted, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordPayment: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = proposal.data.amount;
      var currency = proposal.data.currency;
      var payerSlug = WL.U.slug(proposal.data.payer || '');
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      var matched = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        for (var j = 0; j < q.rewardsPromised.length; j++) {
          var r = q.rewardsPromised[j];
          if (!r.paid && r.amount === amount && r.currency === currency && (!payerSlug || WL.U.slug(r.payer || q.giver) === payerSlug)) {
            r.paid = true;
            r.paidKey = ledger.key;
            q.rewardsPaid.push(r.id);
            matched = q.id;
            root.quests.rewardPayments.push({ questId: q.id, rewardId: r.id, amount: amount, currency: currency, payer: proposal.data.payer || '', key: ledger.key });
            break;
          }
        }
        if (matched) break;
      }
      return { status: matched ? 'APPLIED' : 'NO_MATCHING_PROMISE_RECORDED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'reward_payment_record', questId: matched, key: ledger.key };
    },
    isProtectedObjectiveItem: function (root, targetName, textPreview) {
      var targetId = 'objective_item_' + WL.U.slug(targetName || '');
      if (!targetName || !targetId) return false;
      var lower = WL.U.lower(textPreview || '');
      // If the prose only says the objective was recovered/retrieved/found/completed, treat it as quest progress.
      // Confirmed possession can still be captured by stronger inventory language such as pocket/secure/take/hold.
      if (!/\b(?:recover|recovered|retrieve|retrieved|find|found|bring|brought|complete|completed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:pocket|pocketed|secure|secured|take|takes|took|pick(?:s|ed)? up|hold|holds|held|place(?:s|d)? in your hand|put(?:s)? into your pack|stow|stowed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests && root.quests.questsById || {});
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
        if (q.itemRefs && q.itemRefs.indexOf(targetId) >= 0 && /^(?:offered|active|ready_to_turn_in)$/i.test(q.status || '')) return true;
      }
      return false;
    },
    ensureQuestArrays: function (q) {
      if (!q.objectiveLinks) q.objectiveLinks = { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(q.actorRefs)) q.actorRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.placeRefs)) q.placeRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.itemRefs)) q.itemRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.factionRefs)) q.factionRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.relationships)) q.relationships = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.progress)) q.progress = [];
      return q;
    },
    mergeObjectiveLinks: function (q, links) {
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      links = links || { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      function mergeList(src, dest, objectList) {
        for (var i = 0; i < (src || []).length; i++) {
          var rec = src[i];
          if (!rec || !rec.id) continue;
          if (dest.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) dest.push(rec.id);
          var exists = objectList.some(function (x) { return x.id === rec.id; });
          if (!exists) objectList.push(rec);
        }
      }
      mergeList(links.actors, q.actorRefs, q.objectiveLinks.actors);
      mergeList(links.places, q.placeRefs, q.objectiveLinks.places);
      mergeList(links.items, q.itemRefs, q.objectiveLinks.items);
      mergeList(links.factions, q.factionRefs, q.objectiveLinks.factions);
    },
    linkQuestRelationships: function (root, q, proposal, ledger) {
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.mergeObjectiveLinks(q, proposal.data.links || WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(q.objective, q.giver));
      function pushRel(list, rec) {
        var key = WL.U.slug([rec.kind, rec.questId, rec.actorId || rec.placeId || rec.itemId || rec.factionId].join('|'));
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].relKey === key) return;
        rec.relKey = key;
        list.push(rec);
      }
      if (q.giver && q.giver !== 'Unknown') {
        var giver = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, q.giver);
        pushRel(giver.relationships, { kind: 'quest_giver', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, actorId: giver.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'giver', actorId: giver.id, actorName: giver.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      for (var a = 0; a < q.objectiveLinks.actors.length; a++) {
        var ar = q.objectiveLinks.actors[a];
        var actor = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, ar.name);
        pushRel(actor.relationships, { kind: 'quest_target_actor', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, actorId: actor.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'objective_actor', actorId: actor.id, actorName: actor.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      for (var p = 0; p < q.objectiveLinks.places.length; p++) {
        var pr = q.objectiveLinks.places[p];
        var place = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, pr.name, /wall|gate|bridge|road|barrow/i.test(pr.name) ? 'place' : 'place');
        pushRel(place.relations, { kind: 'quest_objective', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, placeId: place.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(root.places.relations, { kind: 'quest_objective', questId: q.id, placeId: place.id, placeName: place.name, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'objective_place', placeId: place.id, placeName: place.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      WL.U.capArray(q.actorRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.placeRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.itemRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.relationships, 40);
      return q;
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        lines.push(q.title + ' [' + q.status + '] — ' + q.objective);
      }
      return lines.length ? lines : ['(none)'];
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.InventoryEquipmentReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.QuestObjectiveRewardReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.FormTransformationReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.FormTransformationSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.AbilityLifecycleReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.ProgressionReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.ProgressionSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };


  WL.TimeTravelSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'elapsed_time_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.advance(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'route_base_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordRouteBase(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'route_delay_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordRouteDelay(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'deadline_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordDeadline(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'rest_recovery_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.restRecover(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'next_day_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.nextDay(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'time', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_time_event' };
    },
    advanceClock: function (root, minutes) {
      minutes = WL.U.clamp(minutes, 0, 5256000);
      root.time.elapsedMinutes += minutes;
      var total = ((root.time.day - 1) * 1440) + (root.time.hour * 60) + root.time.minute + minutes;
      root.time.day = Math.floor(total / 1440) + 1;
      var rem = total % 1440;
      root.time.hour = Math.floor(rem / 60);
      root.time.minute = rem % 60;
    },
    advance: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var minutes = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.minutes, 1, 5256000);
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, minutes);
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'elapsed', minutes: minutes, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'elapsed', minutes: minutes, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRouteBase: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var data = proposal.data;
      var id = data.targetId || data.routeId || WL.EventScanner.routeId(data.from, data.to);
      var route = root.time.routesById[id];
      if (!route) {
        route = { id: id, from: data.from, to: data.to, baseMinutes: data.minutes, delays: [], history: [], aliases: [data.from + ' -> ' + data.to, data.to + ' -> ' + data.from] };
        root.time.routesById[id] = route;
        root.time.routeIds.push(id);
      } else if (!route.baseMinutes) {
        route.baseMinutes = data.minutes;
      }
      root.time.currentRouteId = id;
      route.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'base', minutes: data.minutes, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(route.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'route_base', routeId: id, baseMinutes: route.baseMinutes, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRouteDelay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = root.time.currentRouteId || 'route_unknown_current_trip';
      var route = root.time.routesById[id];
      if (!route) {
        route = { id: id, from: '', to: '', baseMinutes: 0, delays: [], history: [], aliases: [] };
        root.time.routesById[id] = route;
        root.time.routeIds.push(id);
      }
      route.delays.push({ key: ledger.key, reason: proposal.data.reason || 'Unknown', actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      route.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'delay', reason: proposal.data.reason || 'Unknown', actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0 });
      WL.U.capArray(route.delays, 20);
      WL.U.capArray(route.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'route_delay', routeId: id, actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0, key: ledger.key };
    },
    restRecover: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var data = proposal.data || {};
      var minutes = WL.U.clamp(data.minutes || 480, 1, 1440);
      if (data.overnight) {
        WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceToNextDayTime(root, data.targetHour == null ? 7 : data.targetHour, data.targetMinute || 0);
      } else {
        WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, minutes);
      }
      root.body.hp = Math.min(root.body.maxHp, root.body.hp + WL.U.clamp(data.hpRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      root.body.mp = Math.min(root.body.maxMp, root.body.mp + WL.U.clamp(data.mpRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      root.body.ep = Math.min(root.body.maxEp, root.body.ep + WL.U.clamp(data.epRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      var removed = [];
      if (Array.isArray(root.body.conditions)) {
        var kept = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < root.body.conditions.length; i++) {
          var cond = root.body.conditions[i];
          var cname = WL.U.lower(cond && cond.name || cond && cond.id || '');
          if (/fatigue|fatigued|exhausted|strain/.test(cname) && data.restQuality !== 'poor') removed.push(cond.name || cond.id);
          else kept.push(cond);
        }
        root.body.conditions = kept;
      }
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'rest_recovery', minutes: minutes, overnight: !!data.overnight, quality: data.restQuality || 'ordinary', removedConditions: removed, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'rest_recovery', minutes: minutes, overnight: !!data.overnight, hp: root.body.hp, mp: root.body.mp, ep: root.body.ep, key: ledger.key };
    },
    advanceToNextDayTime: function (root, hour, minute) {
      hour = WL.U.clamp(hour, 0, 23);
      minute = WL.U.clamp(minute || 0, 0, 59);
      var currentAbs = ((root.time.day - 1) * 1440) + (root.time.hour * 60) + root.time.minute;
      var targetAbs = (root.time.day * 1440) + (hour * 60) + minute;
      if (targetAbs <= currentAbs) targetAbs += 1440;
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, targetAbs - currentAbs);
    },
    nextDay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var targetHour = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.hour || 8, 0, 23);
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceToNextDayTime(root, targetHour, proposal.data.minute || 0);
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'next_day', hour: root.time.hour, minute: root.time.minute, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'next_day', day: root.time.day, hour: root.time.hour, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordDeadline: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var label = proposal.data.label || 'Unknown Deadline';
      var id = 'deadline_' + WL.U.hash(label + '|' + proposal.data.task);
      if (!root.time.deadlines.some(function (d) { return d.id === id; })) {
        root.time.deadlines.push({ id: id, label: label, task: proposal.data.task || '', status: 'active', key: ledger.key });
      }
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.deadlines, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'deadline', deadlineId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Day ' + root.time.day + ', ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0') + ' / elapsed ' + root.time.elapsedMinutes + ' min');
      lines.push('Routes: ' + (root.time.routeIds.length ? root.time.routeIds.map(function (id) { var r = root.time.routesById[id]; return (r.from || '?') + ' <-> ' + (r.to || '?') + ' base ' + (r.baseMinutes || 0) + 'm delays ' + (r.delays || []).length; }).join('; ') : '(none)'));
      lines.push('Deadlines: ' + (root.time.deadlines.length ? root.time.deadlines.map(function (d) { return d.label + ' — ' + d.task; }).join('; ') : '(none)'));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.WorldStructureSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'place_definition_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.definePlace(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'current_place_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.setCurrent(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'layout_fact_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordLayout(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'owner_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRelation(root, p, ledger, 'owner');
      if (p.type === 'worker_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRelation(root, p, ledger, 'worker');
      if (p.type === 'room_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRoom(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'quest_place_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordQuestRelation(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'place', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_place_event' };
    },
    ensurePlace: function (root, name, type) {
      name = WL.U.title(name || 'Unknown Place');
      var id = 'place_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      var p = root.places.placesById[id];
      if (!p) {
        p = { id: id, name: name, type: type || 'place', locationHint: '', owners: [], workers: [], layout: {}, layoutHistory: [], contradictions: [], relations: [], history: [] };
        root.places.placesById[id] = p;
        root.places.recent.push(id);
      }
      if (type && (!p.type || p.type === 'place')) p.type = type;
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.recent, 40);
      return p;
    },
    definePlace: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      if (proposal.data.locationHint && !p.locationHint) p.locationHint = proposal.data.locationHint;
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'define', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      if (!root.places.currentId) root.places.currentId = p.id;
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'define', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    setCurrent: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      root.places.currentId = p.id;
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'current', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'current', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordLayout: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var placeId = root.places.currentId;
      if (!placeId) return { status: 'PENDING_NO_CURRENT_PLACE', applied: false, family: 'place', type: 'layout', key: ledger.key };
      var p = root.places.placesById[placeId];
      if (!p) return { status: 'PENDING_NO_CURRENT_PLACE_RECORD', applied: false, family: 'place', type: 'layout', key: ledger.key };
      var fixture = proposal.data.fixture;
      var value = proposal.data.value;
      var old = p.layout[fixture];
      if (old && old !== value) {
        var rec = { key: ledger.key, placeId: placeId, fixture: fixture, oldValue: old, newValue: value, phrase: proposal.textPreview };
        p.contradictions.push(rec);
        root.places.contradictions.push(rec);
        WL.U.capArray(p.contradictions, 20);
        WL.U.capArray(root.places.contradictions, 40);
        return { status: 'CONTRADICTION_FLAGGED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'layout_contradiction', placeId: placeId, key: ledger.key };
      }
      p.layout[fixture] = value;
      p.layoutHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, fixture: fixture, value: value, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.layoutHistory, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'layout', placeId: placeId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger, kind) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, 'place');
      var actorName = proposal.data.actorName || '';
      var list = kind === 'owner' ? p.owners : p.workers;
      if (actorName && list.indexOf(actorName) < 0) list.push(actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: kind, actorName: actorName, placeId: p.id, placeName: p.name };
      p.relations.push(rec);
      root.places.relations.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(p.relations, 30);
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.relations, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: kind + '_relation', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRoom: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, 'building');
      if (!p.rooms) p.rooms = [];
      if (!p.roomIds) p.roomIds = [];
      var roomName = WL.U.title(proposal.data.roomName || 'Room');
      var roomId = proposal.data.roomId || ('room_' + WL.U.slug(p.name + '_' + roomName));
      if (p.roomIds.indexOf(roomId) < 0) {
        p.roomIds.push(roomId);
        p.rooms.push({ id: roomId, name: roomName, parentPlaceId: p.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'room_reference', roomName: roomName, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'room_relation', placeId: p.id, roomId: roomId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordQuestRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: 'quest_objective', questId: proposal.data.questId || '', questTitle: proposal.data.questTitle || '', placeId: p.id, placeName: p.name };
      p.relations.push(rec);
      root.places.relations.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(p.relations, 40);
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.relations, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'quest_place_relation', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var ids = Object.keys(root.places.placesById);
      var lines = [];
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var p = root.places.placesById[ids[i]];
        lines.push(p.name + ' [' + p.type + ']' + (root.places.currentId === p.id ? ' <current>' : '') + ' owners: ' + (p.owners.join(', ') || '-') + ' workers: ' + (p.workers.join(', ') || '-') + ' rooms:' + ((p.roomIds && p.roomIds.length) || 0) + ' rels:' + ((p.relations && p.relations.length) || 0));
      }
      if (root.places.contradictions.length) lines.push('Contradictions: ' + root.places.contradictions.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ActorSocialSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'scene_extra_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.sceneExtra(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'actor_social_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.social(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'actor_place_relation_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.placeRelation(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'actor', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_actor_event' };
    },
    ensureActor: function (root, name) {
      name = WL.U.title(name || 'Unknown Actor');
      var id = 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      var a = root.actors.actorsById[id];
      if (!a) {
        a = { id: id, name: name, role: '', places: [], relationships: [], opinions: [], goals: [], promises: [], debts: [], grudges: [], faction: '', currentPresence: '', lastSeen: '', notes: [], history: [] };
        root.actors.actorsById[id] = a;
        root.actors.referencedIds.push(id);
      }
      if (root.actors.presentIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.actors.presentIds.push(id);
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.presentIds, 40);
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.referencedIds, 80);
      return a;
    },
    sceneExtra: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      root.actors.sceneExtras.push({ key: ledger.key, label: proposal.data.label || 'Extras', note: proposal.data.note || proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.sceneExtras, 40);
      return { status: 'SCENE_EXTRA_ONLY', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'scene_extra', key: ledger.key };
    },
    social: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var a = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, proposal.data.actorName);
      var kind = proposal.data.socialKind || 'social_evidence';
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: kind, note: proposal.data.note || proposal.textPreview };
      if (kind === 'grudge') a.grudges.push(rec);
      else if (kind === 'positive_attitude' || kind === 'negative_attitude') a.opinions.push(rec);
      else a.relationships.push(rec);
      a.history.push(rec);
      root.actors.socialLog.push({ actorId: a.id, kind: kind, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(a.grudges, 20); WL.U.capArray(a.opinions, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.relationships, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.history, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.actors.socialLog, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'social', actorId: a.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    placeRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var a = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, proposal.data.actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, relation: proposal.data.relation || 'related', placeId: proposal.data.placeId || '', placeName: proposal.data.placeName || '' };
      if (rec.placeId && a.places.indexOf(rec.placeId) < 0) a.places.push(rec.placeId);
      a.relationships.push(rec);
      a.history.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(a.places, 20); WL.U.capArray(a.relationships, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.history, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'place_relation', actorId: a.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var ids = Object.keys(root.actors.actorsById);
      var lines = [];
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var a = root.actors.actorsById[ids[i]];
        lines.push(a.name + ' opinions:' + a.opinions.length + ' grudges:' + a.grudges.length + ' places:' + a.places.length);
      }
      if (root.actors.sceneExtras.length) lines.push('Scene extras: ' + root.actors.sceneExtras.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.TimeTravelReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.TimeTravelSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.WorldStructureReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.WorldStructureSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.ActorSocialReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.ActorSocialSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };


  WL.CombatThreatSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'threat_profile_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordThreat(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'group_count_candidate' || p.type === 'reinforcement_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordGroup(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'casualty_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordCasualty(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'all_defeated_claim') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.resolveGroupClaim(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'combat', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_combat_event' };
    },
    estimateThreat: function (label) {
      var l = WL.U.lower(label);
      var level = 5;
      var stats = { atk: 5, def: 5, spd: 5, int: 3, lck: 3 };
      var tags = [];
      if (/rat/.test(l)) { level = 1; stats = { atk: 1, def: 1, spd: 4, int: 1, lck: 2 }; tags.push('small'); }
      if (/goblin/.test(l)) { level = 3; stats = { atk: 4, def: 3, spd: 5, int: 3, lck: 3 }; tags.push('small_group_threat'); }
      if (/wolf/.test(l)) { level = 5; stats = { atk: 8, def: 5, spd: 10, int: 2, lck: 4 }; tags.push('predator'); }
      if (/soldier/.test(l)) { level = 8; stats = { atk: 10, def: 9, spd: 7, int: 5, lck: 4 }; tags.push('armed_human'); }
      if (/village guard/.test(l) || (/guard/.test(l) && !/city|elite|captain/.test(l))) { level = 25; stats = { atk: 28, def: 30, spd: 18, int: 10, lck: 8 }; tags.push('guard_baseline'); }
      if (/city guard|large city guard/.test(l)) { level = 50; stats = { atk: 55, def: 60, spd: 35, int: 20, lck: 15 }; tags.push('large_city_guard'); }
      if (/(elite|veteran|captain).*guard|guard.*(elite|veteran|captain)/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 65); stats = { atk: 72, def: 75, spd: 45, int: 28, lck: 18 }; tags.push('elite_guard'); }
      if (/inferno|fire|flame/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level + 20, 25); stats.atk += 20; stats.def += 8; stats.spd += 5; stats.int += 4; tags.push('elemental_fire'); }
      if (/demon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 60); stats = { atk: 80, def: 60, spd: 55, int: 45, lck: 20 }; tags.push('demonic'); }
      if (/dragon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 90); stats = { atk: 130, def: 120, spd: 80, int: 80, lck: 35 }; tags.push('dragon'); }
      if (/ancient|legendary/.test(l)) { level += 40; stats.atk += 45; stats.def += 45; stats.spd += 25; stats.int += 35; tags.push('legendary'); }
      return { level: level, stats: stats, tags: tags, confidence: 'inferred_from_description', noPlayerRubberBand: true };
    },
    recordThreat: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.creatureName || 'Unknown Threat';
      var id = proposal.data.threatId || ('threat_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      var est = WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat(name);
      var rec = root.combat.threatProfilesById[id];
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { id: id, name: name, level: est.level, stats: est.stats, tags: est.tags, confidence: est.confidence, history: [], noPlayerRubberBand: true };
        root.combat.threatProfilesById[id] = rec;
        root.combat.threatProfileIds.push(id);
      }
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 20);
      root.combat.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'threat_profile', id: id, name: name });
      WL.U.capArray(root.combat.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'threat_profile', threatId: id, level: rec.level, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordGroup: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.groupName || 'Unknown Group';
      var id = proposal.data.groupId || ('group_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      if (!group) {
        group = { id: id, name: name, total: proposal.data.count || 0, remaining: proposal.data.count || 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.combat.groupCountsById[id] = group;
        root.combat.groupIds.push(id);
      } else if (proposal.type === 'reinforcement_candidate') {
        group.total += proposal.data.count || 0;
        group.remaining += proposal.data.count || 0;
      } else if (!group.total) {
        group.total = proposal.data.count || 0;
        group.remaining = proposal.data.count || 0;
      }
      root.combat.activeCombatId = root.combat.activeCombatId || ('combat_' + WL.U.hash(id));
      group.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: proposal.type === 'reinforcement_candidate' ? 'reinforcement' : 'count', count: proposal.data.count, phrase: proposal.textPreview, estimated: proposal.data.estimated === true });
      WL.U.capArray(group.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'group_count', groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    findGroupId: function (root, name) {
      var id = 'group_' + WL.U.slug(name || '');
      if (root.combat.groupCountsById[id]) return id;
      var singular = 'group_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.singularCreature(name || ''));
      if (root.combat.groupCountsById[singular]) return singular;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.combat.groupCountsById);
      var slug = WL.U.slug(name || '');
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) if (keys[i].indexOf(slug) >= 0 || slug.indexOf(keys[i].replace(/^group_/, '')) >= 0) return keys[i];
      return id;
    },
    recordCasualty: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = WL.CombatThreatSystem.findGroupId(root, proposal.data.groupName);
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      if (!group) {
        group = { id: id, name: proposal.data.groupName || 'Unknown Group', total: 0, remaining: 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.combat.groupCountsById[id] = group;
        root.combat.groupIds.push(id);
      }
      var n = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.casualties || 0, 0, 999999);
      group.casualties += n;
      group.remaining = Math.max(0, (group.remaining || 0) - n);
      group.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'casualty', casualties: n, remaining: group.remaining, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(group.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'casualty', groupId: id, casualties: n, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    resolveGroupClaim: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = WL.CombatThreatSystem.findGroupId(root, proposal.data.groupName);
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      var resolution = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.resolution || 'defeated');
      if (group && group.remaining > 0 && !/flee|surrender|dead|killed|slain/.test(resolution)) {
        var block = { key: ledger.key, groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, claim: resolution, phrase: proposal.textPreview, reason: 'remaining_count_blocks_total_defeat' };
        root.combat.blockedClaims.push(block);
        WL.U.capArray(root.combat.blockedClaims, 30);
        return { status: 'BLOCKED_CLAIM', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'all_defeated_blocked', groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
      }
      if (group && /flee|surrender|dead|killed|slain/.test(resolution)) group.remaining = 0;
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'group_resolved', groupId: id, remaining: group ? group.remaining : 0, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = ['No-rubber-band scaling: ' + (root.combat.noRubberBandScaling === true ? 'true' : 'false')];
      root.combat.threatProfileIds.forEach(function (id) { var t = root.combat.threatProfilesById[id]; if (t) lines.push(t.name + ' L' + t.level + ' ATK ' + t.stats.atk + ' DEF ' + t.stats.def + ' SPD ' + t.stats.spd); });
      root.combat.groupIds.forEach(function (id) { var g = root.combat.groupCountsById[id]; if (g) lines.push(g.name + ': ' + g.remaining + '/' + g.total + ' remaining'); });
      if (root.combat.blockedClaims.length) lines.push('Blocked claims: ' + root.combat.blockedClaims.length);
      return lines.length ? lines : ['(none)'];
    }
  };

  WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'economy_price_quote_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.price(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_debt_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.debt(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'crafting_recipe_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.recipe(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_project_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.project(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'warfare_force_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.force(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'warfare_casualty_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.forceCasualty(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'absorption_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.absorption(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'evolution_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.evolution(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'advanced', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_boundary_event' };
    },
    price: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, itemName: p.data.itemName, amount: p.data.amount, currency: p.data.currency, phrase: p.textPreview };
      root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'price_quote', key: ledger.key };
    },
    debt: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, creditor: p.data.creditor, amount: p.data.amount, currency: p.data.currency, paid: false };
      root.advanced.economy.debts.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.debts, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'debt', key: ledger.key };
    },
    recipe: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.recipeId || ('recipe_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.recipeName));
      if (!root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.recipeName, known: true, history: [] };
        root.advanced.crafting.recipeIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'recipe_known', recipeId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    project: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.projectId || ('project_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.projectName));
      if (!root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id]) {
        root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.projectName, status: 'started', progressNotes: [] };
        root.advanced.domain.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id].progressNotes.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_project', projectId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    force: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.forceId || ('force_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.forceName));
      if (!root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.forceName, total: p.data.count || 0, remaining: p.data.count || 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'force_count', count: p.data.count, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'force_count', forceId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    forceCasualty: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.forceId || ('force_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.forceName));
      var f = root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id];
      if (!f) {
        f = { id: id, name: p.data.forceName, total: 0, remaining: 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id] = f;
        root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.push(id);
      }
      var n = WL.U.clamp(p.data.casualties || 0, 0, 999999);
      f.casualties += n;
      f.remaining = Math.max(0, (f.remaining || 0) - n);
      root.advanced.warfare.casualties.push({ key: ledger.key, forceId: id, casualties: n, reason: p.data.reason || '', phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'force_casualty', forceId: id, remaining: f.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    absorption: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.candidateId || ('absorb_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.sourceName));
      if (!root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id] = { id: id, sourceName: p.data.sourceName, status: 'candidate_evidence_only', bounded: true, history: [] };
        root.advanced.absorption.candidateIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'absorption_candidate', candidateId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    evolution: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.candidateId || ('evolve_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.targetForm));
      if (!root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id] = { id: id, targetForm: p.data.targetForm, status: 'candidate_evidence_only', bounded: true, history: [] };
        root.advanced.evolution.candidateIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'evolution_candidate', candidateId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Price quotes: ' + root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.length);
      lines.push('Recipes known: ' + root.advanced.crafting.recipeIds.length);
      lines.push('Domain projects: ' + root.advanced.domain.projectIds.length);
      lines.push('Warfare forces: ' + root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.length);
      lines.push('Absorption candidates: ' + root.advanced.absorption.candidateIds.length);
      lines.push('Evolution candidates: ' + root.advanced.evolution.candidateIds.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.CombatThreatReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.CombatThreatSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.AdvancedBoundaryReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.DirtyQueue = {
    mark: function (root, reducerResults) {
      var anyApplied = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < reducerResults.length; i++) if (reducerResults[i].applied) anyApplied = true;
      if (anyApplied) {
        root.dirty.targets.push('diagnostics');
        for (var d = 0; d < reducerResults.length; d++) {
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'inventory' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('inventory') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('inventory');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'quest' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('quests') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('quests');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'form' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('body') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('body');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'form' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('profile') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('profile');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'ability' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('abilities') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('abilities');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'progression' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('progression') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('progression');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'time' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('time') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('time');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'place' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('places') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('places');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'actor' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('actors') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('actors');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'combat' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('combat') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('combat');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'advanced' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('advanced') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('advanced');
        }
        root.dirty.reasons.push('pipeline_or_owner_update');
        WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.targets, 40);
        WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.reasons, 40);
      }
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 08. OperationLedger and Diagnostics
  // ============================================================
  WL.OperationLedger = {
    makeKey: function (parts) { return WL.U.slug((parts || []).join('|')); },
    has: function (root, key) { return !!(root.operationLedger.keysById[WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([key])]); },
    add: function (root, key, reason) {
      var k = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([key]);
      if (root.operationLedger.keysById[k]) {
        root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips.push({ key: k, reason: reason || 'duplicate', turn: root.runtime.currentTurn });
        WL.U.capArray(root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips, 80);
        return { applied: false, duplicate: true, key: k };
      }
      root.operationLedger.keysById[k] = { key: k, reason: reason || '', turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      root.operationLedger.recentKeys.push(k);
      WL.U.capArray(root.operationLedger.recentKeys, WL.Constants.maxRecentKeys);
      return { applied: true, duplicate: false, key: k };
    }
  };

  WL.Diagnostics = {
    row: function (root, subsystem, level, message, extra) {
      var rec = { turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, subsystem: subsystem || 'system', level: level || 'info', message: WL.U.norm(message).slice(0, 240), extra: extra || null };
      root.diagnostics.audit.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.audit, WL.Constants.maxAuditRows);
      return rec;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 09. ContextSystem skeleton
  // ============================================================
  WL.ContextSystem = {
    build: function (root, baseText) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('[WorldLedger Context — Clean Core Build 7.4]');
      lines.push('Status: installed owner reducers are hardened through Build 7.4; context remains a minimal skeleton until the Context/Card phase.');
      lines.push('Player: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.name) + ' | Race/Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.raceSpecies) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.currentForm));
      lines.push('Resources: HP ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + ', MP ' + root.body.mp + '/' + root.body.maxMp + ', EP ' + root.body.ep + '/' + root.body.maxEp + '.');
      lines.push('Progression: ' + WL.ProgressionSystem.summary(root)[0] + ' | ' + WL.ProgressionSystem.summary(root)[4]);
      lines.push('Time: Day ' + root.time.day + ' ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0') + ' | Place: ' + (root.places.currentId ? root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name : '(unknown)'));
      lines.push('Actors present: ' + (root.actors.presentIds.length || 0) + ' | Known places: ' + Object.keys(root.places.placesById).length);
      lines.push('Pipeline: scanner/proposal/validator/reducer/ledger installed. Quest/world/actor relationship hardening is active; context/cards/full acceptance remain blocked.');
      lines.push('Canon rule: context is read-only; cards/commands/context do not grant mechanics.');
      return lines.join('\n');
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 10. CommandRouter and CommandRenderer
  // ============================================================
  WL.CommandRouter = {
    route: function (text, root) {
      if (!root.features.commandsEnabled) return text;
      var name = WL.U.commandName(text);
      var renderer = WL.CommandRenderer[name] || WL.CommandRenderer.unknown;
      return renderer(root, WL.U.commandArgs(text), name);
    }
  };

  WL.CommandRenderer = {
    header: function (name, status) {
      return '[WorldLedger]\nCommand: /' + name + '\nStatus: ' + (status || 'INFO') + '\n';
    },
    unknown: function (root, args, name) {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header(name || 'unknown', 'UNKNOWN') + 'Unknown command. Use /help.\n';
    },
    help: function () {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('help', 'BUILD_PHASE') +
        'WorldLedger Clean Core Build 7.4 hardens quest/objective, actor, and world relationship flow before Context/Card mirrors while preserving the clean runtime pipeline.\n' +
        'Available commands: /help, /version, /owners, /sourceaudit, /schemaaudit, /runtimecheck, /pipelinecheck, /parser, /proposal, /validator, /profile, /body, /inventory, /quests, /questcheck, /relationshipwebcheck, /abilities, /skills, /talents, /passives, /progression, /domains, /time, /places, /actors, /stateaudit, /build3check, /build31check, /build4check, /build5check, /build51check, /build6check, /build7check, /build71check, /build72check, /build73check, /build74check, /profilecheck, /racecheck, /formcheck, /abilitycheck, /progressioncheck, /growthmatrixcheck, /domainxpcheck, /costcheck, /timecheck, /placecheck, /actorcheck, /worldcheck, /combatcheck, /threatcheck, /groupcombatcheck, /advancedboundarycheck, /economycheck, /craftingcheck, /domaincheck, /warfarecheck, /absorptioncheck, /evolutioncheck, /releasecheck.\n' +
        'Pipeline is active: scanner -> proposal -> validator -> reducer -> ledger -> dirty/diagnostics. Inventory/currency denial, time/rest/body hardening, and quest/world/actor relationship hardening are active; context/card systems remain blocked.\n';

    version: function () {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('version', 'BUILD_PHASE') +

      var lines = [WL.CommandRenderer.header('owners', 'INFO') + 'Owner manifest:'];

      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var k = keys[i];
        lines.push('- ' + k + ': ' + WL.OwnerManifest[k].owner + ' / ' + WL.OwnerManifest[k].status);
      }
      return lines.join('\n') + '\n';

      lines.push('Race / Species: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.raceSpecies));

      var rows = [];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-001', root.schemaVersion === WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, 'schema version matches clean-core Build 7.1'));

      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-005', root.features.gameplayReducersEnabled === true && root.features.inventoryQuestReducersEnabled === true, 'critical inventory/quest owner reducers active'));

      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-009', !!root.progression && !!root.progression.statXp && !!root.progression.domainXp && root.progression.buildIdentity.noRubberBandScaling === true, 'progression/stat/domain buckets exist with no-rubber-band flag'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-010', root.features.abilityKeysCostHardeningEnabled === true && !!root.abilityCosts.overchargeRisksById, 'Build 5.1 Keys/cost-pressure schema fields exist'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-011', root.features.combatThreatReducersEnabled === true && !!root.combat.threatProfilesById && !!root.combat.groupCountsById, 'Build 7 combat/threat schema fields exist'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-012', root.features.advancedBoundaryReducersEnabled === true && !!root.advanced && !!root.advanced.economy && !!root.advanced.absorption, 'Build 7 advanced boundary schema fields exist'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SCHEMA-013', root.features.inventoryCurrencyDenialHardeningEnabled === true, 'Build 7.2 inventory/currency denial hardening flag active'));
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('schemaaudit', rows, 'Schema pipeline audit.');

      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('STATE-010', !!root.combat && root.combat.noRubberBandScaling === true && !!root.combat.groupCountsById, 'combat/threat state exists with no-rubber-band flag'));

      rows = rows.concat(WL.CombatAdvancedSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CoreFlowHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.InventoryCurrencyDenialSelfTest.rows());
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-STATE-001', root.build.buildVersion === WL.BUILD_VERSION, 'build identity is clean-core Build 7.4 while preserving Build 7.2 checks'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-FEATURE-001', root.features.inventoryCurrencyDenialHardeningEnabled === true, 'Build 7.2 inventory/currency denial hardening feature flag active'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-RELEASE-001', WL.STATUS === 'BUILD_PHASE_ONLY_NOT_RELEASE', 'not pretending to be final release'));
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('build72check', rows, 'Build 7.2 inventory/currency/quote-denial hardening gate preserved under Build 7.4.');
    },
    build73check: function (root) {
      var rows = [];
      rows = rows.concat(WL.SourceAudit.runtimeRows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.PipelineSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.ProfileSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.FormSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AbilityProgressionSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeWorldActorSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CombatAdvancedSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CoreFlowHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.InventoryCurrencyDenialSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeRestBodyHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-STATE-001', root.build.buildVersion === WL.BUILD_VERSION, 'build identity is clean-core Build 7.3'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-FEATURE-001', root.features.timeRestBodyHardeningEnabled === true, 'Build 7.3 time/rest/body hardening feature flag active'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-RELEASE-001', WL.STATUS === 'BUILD_PHASE_ONLY_NOT_RELEASE', 'not pretending to be final release'));
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('build73check', rows, 'Build 7.3 time/rest/body hardening gate preserved under Build 7.4.');
    },
    build74check: function (root) {
      var rows = [];
      rows = rows.concat(WL.SourceAudit.runtimeRows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.PipelineSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.ProfileSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.FormSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AbilityProgressionSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeWorldActorSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CombatAdvancedSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CoreFlowHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.InventoryCurrencyDenialSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeRestBodyHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.QuestWorldRelationshipSelfTest.rows());
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B74-STATE-001', root.build.buildVersion === WL.BUILD_VERSION, 'build identity is clean-core Build 7.4'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B74-FEATURE-001', root.features.questWorldRelationshipHardeningEnabled === true, 'Build 7.4 quest/world/actor relationship hardening feature flag active'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B74-RELEASE-001', WL.STATUS === 'BUILD_PHASE_ONLY_NOT_RELEASE', 'not pretending to be final release'));
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('build74check', rows, 'Build 7.4 quest/actor/world relationship hardening gate before Context/Card systems.');
    },
    releasecheck: function (root) {
      var rows = [];
      rows = rows.concat(WL.SourceAudit.runtimeRows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.PipelineSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.ProfileSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.FormSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AbilityProgressionSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeWorldActorSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CombatAdvancedSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.CoreFlowHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.InventoryCurrencyDenialSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.TimeRestBodyHardeningSelfTest.rows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.QuestWorldRelationshipSelfTest.rows());
      rows.push(WL.Assert.warn('REL-001', 'Build 7.4 is intentionally not releasable; core owner flows are hardened but context/cards/full acceptance remain blocked.'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.block('REL-002', 'Release blocked until later clean-core phases install context/card mirrors, flat final acceptance, source audit hardening, and release candidate audit.'));
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('releasecheck', rows, 'Release check is honest: Build 7.4 is blocked by design.');
    },
    renderObjects: function (items, title) {
      var arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
      var lines = [title + ': ' + arr.length];
      for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(arr.length, 12); i++) {
        var item = arr[i];
        lines.push('- ' + (item.status || item.family || 'row') + ' ' + (item.family || '') + '/' + (item.type || '') + ' ' + (item.targetName || item.reason || item.textPreview || ''));
      }
      return lines.join('\n');
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 11. Assertions / SourceAudit / SelfTest
  // ============================================================
  WL.Assert = {
    pass: function (id, condition, label) { return { status: condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL', id: id, label: label }; },
    warn: function (id, label) { return { status: 'WARN', id: id, label: label }; },
    block: function (id, label) { return { status: 'BLOCKED', id: id, label: label }; }
  };


  WL.InventoryCurrencyDenialSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var r;
      function names(root) { return root.inventory.ownedIds.map(function (id) { return root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].displayName || id; }); }
      function has(root, name) { return names(root).some(function (x) { return WL.U.lower(x) === WL.U.lower(name); }); }

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You pick up the idea of a brass key while reading the map, not an actual key.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-DENY-001', names(r).length === 0, 'idea/metaphor pickup does not create an item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'A sign reads: "You pick up the red healing potion." It is only an example sentence.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-DENY-002', names(r).length === 0, 'written sign/example pickup does not create an item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira says, "You pick up the minor healing vial," as a grammar lesson. Nothing changes hands.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-DENY-003', names(r).length === 0, 'NPC lesson/quoted pickup does not create an item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-MIXED-001', has(r, 'Red Healing Potion') && !has(r, 'Jeweled Crown'), 'mixed refusal/handoff grants only the confirmed item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You retrieve the black gate key from the drawer.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-RETRIEVE-001', has(r, 'Black Gate Key'), 'retrieve from drawer counts as confirmed acquisition'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You recover your bearings after the fall.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-RETRIEVE-002', names(r).length === 0, 'recovering bearings does not create inventory'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'He counts out seventy dull copper coins into a small worn leather pouch. He hands it to you.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-CURRENCY-001', r.inventory.currency.copper === 70 && names(r).length === 0, 'multi-clause pouch handoff grants currency, not malformed item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You take 70 copper coins and place them into your pocket.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-CURRENCY-002', r.inventory.currency.copper === 70 && names(r).length === 0, 'taking counted coins into pocket grants currency, not coin item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot(); r.inventory.currency.copper = 70;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You count out thirty copper coins from your pouch. The woman sweeps them into a box.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-CURRENCY-003', r.inventory.currency.copper === 40, 'counting out copper spends/removes currency'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot(); r.inventory.currency.gold = 10;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You buy the iron dagger for 8 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-PURCHASE-001', r.inventory.currency.gold === 2 && has(r, 'Iron Dagger'), 'confirmed purchase spends currency and adds item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The iron dagger costs 8 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B72-QUOTE-001', names(r).length === 0 && r.inventory.currency.gold === 0 && r.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.length === 1, 'price quote records quote only'));

      return rows;
    }
  };
